Files additively — the project plus its first next-action — with a single per-project confirmation, then a light skippable goal-link nudge. The capture-time twin of /self-org:review-project. ([Integral-Productivity/holacracy-claude-plugin](https://github.com/Integral-Productivity/holacracy-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/capture-project.md))

- **capture-references**: Pull references (URLs or topics) from the web, summarize each with a freshness format, and append them to the project's REFERENCES.md for later tasks. Use when the user persists project-level references, supplies URLs, has a reference that outlives the task, or seeds REFERENCES.md. Do not use when the project folder is missing (run project-bootstrap), the input is task-internal, a codebase observation (use capture-observation), or a decision (use decision-interview or direction-adjust).
